        # Implementation Plan: Migrate reigh-worker execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy

## Overview
Author a migration plan document at `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` covering the cutover of `reigh-worker`'s in-process generation backend from vendored `Wan2GP/wgp.py` (orchestrated via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) to `vibecomfy` (Python toolkit around ComfyUI, with `VibeWorkflow` IR, `ready_templates/`, embedded + RunPod runtimes in `vibecomfy/runtime/`).

Key constraints discovered while scoping (verified against the working tree):
- **Memory profiles** are non-negotiable. Today: `--wgp-profile {1..5}` in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:556-566` mapping to mmgp's `force_profile_no` / `default_profile`, plus per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`. VibeComfy already exposes lower-level memory controls in `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:46-49` (`SessionConfig.vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`). Translation paths verified: `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` builds an embedded ComfyUI `Configuration` (sets `highvram`/`lowvram`/`normalvram`, `reserve_vram`, `cache_classic`/`cache_none`/`cache_lru`, `disable_smart_memory`); `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` builds the managed-server CLI argv (`--highvram`/`--lowvram`/`--normalvram`, `--reserve-vram`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic`/`--cache-none`/`--cache-lru N`). The 1–5 tier abstraction is missing — it must layer **on top of** the existing `SessionConfig` surface, not replace it.
- **Default profile in production today is 1, not 3.** Profile-3 default appears in dev/local paths only: `start_worker.bat:14`, `scripts/live_test/main.py:27`, `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27`. The orchestrator's RunPod startup template at `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463` passes `--wgp-profile 1`. The migration baseline measurements must distinguish per environment.
- **Task surface to preserve** comes from the union of three sources, not just `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`:
  - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` (direct-queue catalog: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `flux`, `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `hunyuan`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`, `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `inpaint_frames`, `generate_video`, `join_clips_segment`).
  - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:1442-1511` dispatch handlers (orchestrated/specialized): `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
  - `source/core/log/display_names.py:8-39` display aliases (`rife_interpolate` is the friendly alias; the runtime task type is `rife_interpolate_images`).
- **Existing ComfyUI integration** must be audited, not duplicated. `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` and `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` already provide a lazy-launched ComfyUI server, an HTTP client, and the `comfy` task-type dispatch. The migration retires the bespoke launcher in favor of `vibecomfy.runtime.session.EmbeddedSession` and refactors `handle_comfy_task` to delegate via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`.
- **Coupling beyond generation**: `WanOrchestrator` handles dynamic model definition load, LoRA setup, Uni3C ControlNet caching, RIFE temporal interpolation, Qwen prompt expander, Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotators, save-video helpers — re-exported through `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` and `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`.
- **Orchestrator coupling extends beyond `--wgp-profile`**: `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh` references Wan2GP in three discrete places that all need cleanup at Sprint 8: line 174 (`FALLBACK_DIR="$WORKSPACE_DIR/Headless-Wan2GP"` legacy worker-directory fallback used at lines 179-183), lines 267-292 (Wan2GP submodule reconciliation block: stale-clone removal at 267-277, missing-submodule hard-fail at 284-292), and line 463 (`--wgp-profile 1`).
- **VibeComfy ready templates** at `vibecomfy/ready_templates/{image,video,edit,audio}/` count **50 `.py` files** by live `find` count (the README.md figure of 46 is stale). Coverage gap confirmed by directory inspection: **no Hunyuan template** exists in `ready_templates/video/` (only an `EmptyHunyuanLatentVideo` node reference inside Wan templates).

The deliverable is a planning markdown doc; no code changes in this phase. The executor will write to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Frame the document and write metadata header (`docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open with H1 `# reigh-worker → VibeComfy Migration Plan`, status banner ("Draft, 2026-05-05"), one-paragraph summary, and a clickable TOC.
2. State goals (preserve external worker contract: queue payloads, output shapes, per-task SLOs, memory-profile guarantees) and explicit non-goals (no Supabase queue schema rework, no orchestrator algorithmic changes beyond image/runtime swap, no `reigh-app` UI/API changes).
3. Reference authoritative source paths up front: `reigh-worker/source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}`, `reigh-worker/source/core/log/display_names.py`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/{run.py,session.py,server.py,client.py}`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`, `vibecomfy/docs/runtime_surface.md`.

### Step 2: Audit current reigh-worker → Wan2GP integration (Section 1)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document runtime entry path: `run_worker.py` → `source/runtime/worker/server.py` (CLI flags `--wgp-attention-mode`, `--wgp-compile`, `--wgp-profile`, `--wgp-vae-config`, `--wgp-boost`, `--wgp-transformer-quantization`, `--wgp-transformer-dtype-policy`, `--wgp-text-encoder-quantization`, `--wgp-vae-precision`, `--wgp-mixed-precision`, `--wgp-preload-policy`, `--wgp-preload`) → `import wgp as wgp_mod` (`server.py:551`) → `HeadlessTaskQueue` (`task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) → `WanOrchestrator` (`models/wgp/orchestrator.py`).
2. Enumerate memory-profile system: profile values 1–5 with display names from `server.py:605-609` ("Max Performance", "High RAM", "Balanced", "Conservative", "Minimum"); mmgp `force_profile_no`/`default_profile` set at `server.py:556-558`; per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`. **Default-profile-by-environment table** (verified):
   - Dev local Windows: `start_worker.bat:14` → `--wgp-profile 3`.
   - Dev local live-tests: `scripts/live_test/main.py:27`, `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27` → `--wgp-profile 3`.
   - Production RunPod orchestrator: `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463` → `--wgp-profile 1`.
   The plan's Sprint 0 baseline must measure both profile 1 and profile 3 paths because production and dev diverge.
3. Tabulate the **full task surface** in one table (cols: task_type, source-of-truth file, default_model, dispatch_path, current handler module, output shape, alias, notes). Build the union of `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`, the 14 dispatch keys at `task_registry.py:1442-1511` (`travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`), and friendly aliases from `display_names.py:8-39`. The "alias for" column ensures `rife_interpolate` (alias) does not leak forward; the runtime name is `rife_interpolate_images`.
4. Diagram queue/dispatch and the **two adapter seams**: (a) direct-queue conversion `TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task` → `db_task_to_generation_task(params, task_id, task_type, context["wan2gp_path"], context["debug_mode"])` → `HeadlessTaskQueue.submit_task`; (b) specialized handlers receiving `context["task_queue"]` and enqueueing child WGP tasks. Capture the lazy bootstrap pattern in `task_handlers/queue/wgp_init.py`.
5. Document model lifecycle: `load_model_impl`/`unload_model_impl`/`load_missing_model_definition` in `source/models/wgp/model_ops.py`; dynamic JSON model defs at `Wan2GP/profiles/*` and `Wan2GP/defaults/*`; runtime-mutable hooks in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/runtime_registry.py`; LoRA flow (`source/models/wgp/lora_setup.py`, `wgp_patches.py:389-445`).
6. Document vendor surface re-exported via `wgp_bridge` + `vendor_imports`: Qwen prompt expander, Canny/DepthV2/Flow/Pose annotators, `flow_viz`, `run_rife_temporal_interpolation`, `load_uni3c_controlnet`, Wan2GP `save_video`, shared LoRA utils, Qwen family handler, Qwen main module. Each row gets a "VibeComfy equivalent / gap" cell.
7. Document the **existing ComfyUI integration**: `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` (lazy-launches local ComfyUI subprocess for the `comfy` task type), `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` (`ComfyUIManager`, `ComfyUIClient`, `COMFY_PATH`, `COMFY_PORT`), the `comfy` dispatch branch at `task_registry.py:1507-1510`, and tests that import these modules. Decision (Step 4 §7): retire the bespoke launcher; refactor `handle_comfy_task` to delegate via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`.
8. Document error paths and telemetry: `source/models/wgp/error_extraction.py`, `core/log/core.py:59,112` (mmgp version warnings), `task_handlers/queue/memory_cleanup.py`, `cleanup_memory_after_task`, `model_patch_session.py`, `status_wait.py`, memory-stats logging in `wgp/generators/output.py`.
9. Document orchestrator coupling at the runtime layer (every Wan2GP touchpoint that must change at cutover): `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:174` (`FALLBACK_DIR="$WORKSPACE_DIR/Headless-Wan2GP"` and the elif fallback at lines 179-183), lines 267-292 (Wan2GP submodule reconciliation block — stale-clone removal at 267-277, missing-submodule hard-fail at 284-292), line 463 (`--wgp-profile 1`); plus `gpu_orchestrator/Dockerfile` Wan2GP install steps; `pyproject.toml` pinning `mmgp==3.7.6`; `--queue-workers` for capacity; pod disk 50 GB sized for one Wan2GP family at a time.

### Step 3: Audit VibeComfy capabilities (Section 2)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document `VibeWorkflow` IR (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/workflow.py`): `add_node`/`connect`/`disconnect`/`replace_edge`/`finalize_metadata`/`compile("api")` per `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`. Rule: changes-handles → template, decorates-handles → patch.
2. Document `ready_templates/` organization. Use the live `find vibecomfy/ready_templates -name '*.py' | wc -l` count of **50** (verified) and explicitly note the README's "46" figure is stale. Map known templates to each direct-queue task type (e.g., `image/z_image.py` ↔ `z_image_turbo`, `image/qwen_image_2512.py` ↔ `qwen_image_2512`, `video/wan_t2v.py` ↔ `t2v`/`t2v_22`, `video/wan_i2v.py` ↔ `i2v`/`i2v_22`, `video/ltx2_3_t2v.py` ↔ `ltx2`, `video/wanvideo_wrapper_*` for VACE / FLF2V / fun-control / camera). Mark missing-template gaps; the largest is `hunyuan` (no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*`).
3. Document the runtime: `run_embedded`/`run_embedded_sync` (`runtime/run.py`) over `EmbeddedSession` (`runtime/session.py`); `run`/`run_sync` against an external Comfy server (`runtime/{client.py,server.py}`). Capture `_build_schema_provider` schema-gate warning behavior.
4. Document **existing memory controls** on `SessionConfig` (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:46-49`): `vram_policy: str = "auto"` (`high|low|normal|auto`), `reserve_vram_gb: float | None`, `cache_policy: str = "smart"` (`smart|classic|none|lru:N`), `disable_smart_memory: bool`. Translation paths (corrected citations):
   - `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` builds an embedded ComfyUI `Configuration` dict — sets `highvram`/`lowvram`/`normalvram`, `reserve_vram`, `cache_classic`/`cache_none`/`cache_lru`, `disable_smart_memory`, then merges `config.extra` and `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env JSON, calls `default_configuration().update(values)`.
   - `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` builds the managed-server CLI argv — appends `--highvram`/`--lowvram`/`--normalvram`, `--reserve-vram <gb>`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic`/`--cache-none`/`--cache-lru <N>`, plus `--port`.
   The migration's profile abstraction must layer on these knobs at both surfaces.
5. Document validation: `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/schema/{validate.py,cache.py,format.py,provider.py}` plus `vibecomfy doctor`.
6. Document RunPod path: VibeComfy delegates pod lifecycle to `runpod-lifecycle` via `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` (list/status/terminate/gpu-types). Remote execution still goes through `run(server_url=...)`. For reigh-worker, `EmbeddedSession` is the analogue of vendored `wgp.py`.
7. Document explicit gaps: no 1–5 profile tier abstraction (only the lower-level knobs in §4); no LoRA-key sanitizer equivalent (cf. `wgp_patches.py:389-445`); no Uni3C ControlNet cache; no RIFE temporal interpolation helper; no Qwen prompt expander wrapper; no Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotator re-exports; no Wan2GP `save_video` callable; no Hunyuan ready_template. Each becomes a row in the Section 3 gap table.

### Step 4: Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (Section 3)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Author "Parity Gap Matrix" table (cols: capability, current Wan2GP location, VibeComfy current state, required work, P-priority, owner-repo, target sprint).
2. Specify **memory-profile abstraction (P0)** as a thin layer over the existing `SessionConfig` knobs:
   - New `vibecomfy.runtime.profile` module with `MemoryProfile` enum (`MAX_PERFORMANCE=1`, `HIGH_RAM=2`, `BALANCED=3`, `CONSERVATIVE=4`, `MINIMUM=5`) and a `MemoryProfile.to_session_overrides()` returning a partial dict that overlays into `SessionConfig` fields (`vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`) plus optional per-template policy hooks (loader class swaps, GGUF substitution, tile sizes — same shape as the LTX low-VRAM patch policy in `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`). Both translation surfaces (`_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` and `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678`) consume the resulting `SessionConfig` unchanged.
   - Concrete starting mapping (Sprint-1 tuned): MAX_PERFORMANCE → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; HIGH_RAM → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="lru:32"`; BALANCED → `vram_policy="normal"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; CONSERVATIVE → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="classic"`, `reserve_vram_gb=2.0`; MINIMUM → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="none"`, `disable_smart_memory=True`, `reserve_vram_gb=4.0`.
   - `EmbeddedSession`/`SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata` accept `profile: MemoryProfile`; per-call `override_profile` arg on `run_embedded` matches `wgp_params.py` semantics (Open Question Q2 covers whether overrides require process restart).
   - Acceptance: per-profile parity smoke tests for representative model per family (Wan T2V, Wan I2V, LTX2, Qwen, Z-Image, Flux), measuring peak VRAM and wall-clock vs Wan2GP profile-1 (production) and profile-3 (dev) baselines.
3. Specify **task-type → VibeComfy template registry**: lives in `reigh-worker` at `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (only genuinely new file). Covers the union task surface (direct-queue catalog plus orchestrated-handler internal sub-tasks). "No template yet" rows block cutover.
4. Specify **missing-template gaps with explicit P-priority and go/no-go**:
   - **Hunyuan (P0 for Cohort D / Sprint 4)** — no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` exists. Cohort D cutover **must not start** until a Hunyuan ready template ships. Owner: VibeComfy maintainer; target: Sprint 4.
   - Any further gaps surfaced by Step 3 §2 mapping table get the same explicit go/no-go gate per cohort (e.g., `qwen_image_hires`, `annotated_image_edit` — executor verifies during write-up).
5. Specify **vendor-utility shims**: RIFE keeps vendored under `reigh-worker/source/media/`; Uni3C as VibeComfy patch on Wan2.2 templates or load directly inside handler; Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow pre-process before workflow build (preferred); Qwen prompt expander pre-process; LoRA-key sanitizer becomes a VibeComfy patch over `LoraLoader` nodes.
6. Specify **dynamic-model-definition** decision: recommend build-time freeze (ship templates upfront), call out as Open Question Q1.
7. **Decide the fate of `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` + `comfy_utils.py`**: refactor `comfy_handler.py` to delegate to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded` and `vibecomfy.runtime.client.ComfyClient`; retire `comfy_utils.py` (`ComfyUIManager`/`ComfyUIClient`); add sibling `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py`. Tests under `tests/` that import the retired modules are migrated.
8. Specify **observability shim**: translate `RunResult` (`run_id`, `prompt_id`, `outputs`, `metadata_path`, `log_path`) into existing telemetry (heartbeat logs, `system_logs` table, `source/core/log/debug_card.py`).

### Step 5: Sprint-by-sprint migration plan (Section 4)
**Scope:** Large
1. Sprint table (≤2 wk each), each listing: goals, shippable artifacts, exit criteria, owner, risk, rollback move.
   - **Sprint 0 (1 wk)** Discovery freeze + benchmark harness via `scripts/run_worker_matrix.py` + `scripts/live_test/`. Output: `reigh-worker/docs/migration-baselines.md` with separate baselines for production profile-1 (orchestrator default) and dev profile-3 (`start_worker.bat`/live_test). Also produce live task-surface inventory (`task_registry.py:1442-1511` dispatch keys ∪ `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`) and live VibeComfy template inventory (`find vibecomfy/ready_templates -name '*.py'`).
   - **Sprint 1 (2 wk)** VibeComfy memory-profile MVP (Step 4 §2): `MemoryProfile` enum + `SessionConfig` overlay + per-call `override_profile`. Exit: profiles 1–5 round-trip on `image/z_image` and `video/wan_t2v` with measured VRAM/wall-clock at parity-or-better than `--wgp-profile {1..5}` for both production-profile-1 and dev-profile-3 reference points.
   - **Sprint 2 (2 wk)** Adapter shim in reigh-worker — refactor existing `source/models/comfy/`:
     - Rewrite `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` to call VibeComfy via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`.
     - Add `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (task_type → template_id + parameter adapter) covering both adapter seams.
     - Retire `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py`; migrate test imports.
     - Feature flag `REIGH_BACKEND={wgp|comfy}` read in `task_queue.py`/`wgp_init.py` and threaded through to **both** seam (a) (`_handle_direct_queue_task`) and seam (b) (nested handler-created child tasks via `context["task_queue"]`).
     - Wire safest tasks first: `z_image_turbo`, `qwen_image_2512`, `t2v`. Exit: feature-flagged Comfy path runs end-to-end in dev for those three, including a smoke run through a `travel_segment` orchestrator that enqueues a `t2v` child task.
   - **Sprint 3 (2 wk)** Dual-execution + comparison harness `scripts/dual_run_compare.py` (image hash, video frame-pHash, frame count, dimensions, audio length, latency, VRAM, OOM count). Nightly across the Sprint 0 matrix. Exit: green report ≥80% of task types.
   - **Sprint 4 (2 wk)** Wan-family + Hunyuan parity. Wan: `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`. **Hunyuan (P0 for this sprint, no go without it):** ship `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` in VibeComfy, including memory-profile mapping. Exit: dual-run parity green for Wan family + Hunyuan template runnable with each profile.
   - **Sprint 5 (2 wk)** LTX, Flux, Qwen parity + move pre-processing pipeline (Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow). Exit: dual-run parity green for remaining direct-queue tasks.
   - **Sprint 6 (2 wk)** Orchestrated handlers covering both seams: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`. Exit: full union task surface runnable through Comfy backend on both adapter seams.
   - **Sprint 7 (2 wk)** Canary in production by `task_type` cohort using server-side selector. Hold each cohort 48 h before promoting next.
   - **Sprint 8 (1 wk)** Wan2GP removal (full enumeration in Step 7 §3). Includes cleanup of orchestrator startup template at lines 174 + 267-292 + 463 (not just 463).
2. Critical path callout: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3, then 4/5 in parallel, then 6 → 7 → 8.

### Step 6: Per-task-type cutover order with rationale (Section 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Cohort table covering the union task surface:
   - **A** lowest risk, image-only deterministic: `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_2512`, `flux`, `wan_2_2_t2i`.
   - **B** medium, image edits: `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`.
   - **C** medium-high, video gen: `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `generate_video`.
   - **D** high, VACE + Hunyuan: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `hunyuan` — gated on Hunyuan ready_template existing in `vibecomfy/ready_templates/video/`.
   - **E** highest, orchestrated + raw-Comfy: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
2. Canary mechanism: server-side `backend_for_task_type` map read at task claim time, with explicit "legacy" fallback while migrating. Cohort E tasks must respect both the parent task's backend selection AND the child-task seam — covered by Sprint 6.

### Step 7: Rollback plan, telemetry/observability, final removal (Sections 6–8)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Rollback: `REIGH_BACKEND` flag at worker process level via `--backend wgp|comfy` plus per-task-type override; both stacks remain in worker image until Sprint 8; orchestrator passes flag through `worker_startup.template.sh`. Trigger conditions: latency > p95 baseline by N%, new error-class spike, output-divergence rate > threshold. Document signals (`heartbeat_guardian` error classes, `system_logs` filters, dual-run delta thresholds). Adapter scope explicitly covers both seams: direct-queue conversion (`_handle_direct_queue_task`) and nested handler-created child tasks routed via `context["task_queue"]`.
2. Telemetry: add `backend` and `template_id` log labels; emit `vibecomfy.run_id` and `comfy.prompt_id` alongside task id; mirror VRAM logging from `wgp/generators/output.py:log_memory_stats` to Comfy path; capture `RunResult.log_path` into `source/core/log/debug_card.py`.
3. Final Wan2GP removal checklist (Sprint 8), explicitly enumerated for both repos:
   - **reigh-worker:** `Wan2GP/` submodule and `.gitmodules` entry; `source/runtime/wgp_*`; `source/models/wgp/`; WGP-override block at `source/runtime/worker/server.py:544-595`; all `--wgp-*` CLI flags (rename to `--vibecomfy-profile`); `mmgp==3.7.6` from `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock`; `tests/test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py` mmgp shims; `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` (retired in Sprint 2 if still present).
   - **reigh-worker-orchestrator (`gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh` cleanup):**
     - **Line 174 + lines 179-183** — remove `FALLBACK_DIR="$WORKSPACE_DIR/Headless-Wan2GP"` and the `elif [ -d "$FALLBACK_DIR" ]` branch that points `WORKDIR` at the legacy Headless-Wan2GP directory.
     - **Lines 267-292** — remove the entire Wan2GP submodule reconciliation block: stale-clone removal at 267-277 (`if [ -f .gitmodules ] && grep -q 'path = Wan2GP' ...`) and missing-submodule hard-fail at 284-292 (`refusing to start worker`).
     - **Line 463** — change `--wgp-profile 1` to `--vibecomfy-profile 1` (or remove if profile selection moves to env).
   - **reigh-worker-orchestrator other:** `gpu_orchestrator/Dockerfile` (drop Wan2GP install), `requirements.txt` / `requirements-dev.txt` (drop mmgp transitively), env-example updates.

### Step 8: Open questions, assumptions, risks/mitigations (Section 9)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open questions:
   - Q1 Static vs dynamic VibeComfy template authoring (mirror of `load_missing_model_definition`)?
   - Q2 Does ComfyUI's per-process memory model accept the proposed 5-tier `MemoryProfile`→`SessionConfig` overlay cleanly, or do some profile transitions require a Comfy process restart?
   - Q3 Output bit-exact reproduction is unrealistic — what divergence threshold (frame-pHash distance, perceptual delta, audio length tolerance) defines "pass" in dual-run harness?
   - Q4 Pre-process annotators host: `reigh-worker` or VibeComfy plugins under `vibecomfy_extras/`?
   - Q5 RunPod startup-time differences (image size, ComfyUI cold-boot) significant enough to change worker amortization or `disk_size_gb`?
   - Q6 LoRA-key sanitizer (`wgp_patches.py:389-445`) — mmgp-specific or does ComfyUI's LoRA loader trip too?
   - Q7 RIFE / Uni3C ControlNet — keep vendored in reigh-worker or wrap as VibeComfy custom nodes?
   - Q8 Hunyuan ready_template authorship: who builds it, against which reference workflow, on what timeline (Sprint 4 hard gate)?
   - Q9 Fate of the existing `comfy` task type post-migration: retire / pass-through / repurpose as raw-API escape hatch?
   - Q10 Should the dev default profile be aligned with prod (move `start_worker.bat` and live-test scripts from profile 3 → 1) before cutover, to keep baseline measurements consistent?
2. Assumptions: queue contracts and Supabase schema unchanged; orchestrator continues single-GPU pods; `reigh-app` unaffected; `vibecomfy` is canonical home for new templates; the 5-tier display contract (`server.py:605-609`) is preserved.
3. Risks + mitigations: missing 1–5 profile tier (Sprint 1 P0, layered on `SessionConfig`); embedded ComfyUI cold-boot dominating short-job latency (long-lived `EmbeddedSession`); template catalog drift (template-routing tests + live-filesystem inventory); Hunyuan template absence (Sprint 4 hard gate); LoRA divergence (golden-output corpus); pod disk too small during dual-run (Sprint 0 baselining + bump `disk_size_gb`); orchestrated child-task seam missed by adapter (Sprint 2 explicit dual-seam wiring + Sprint 6 coverage); existing `source/models/comfy` integration drift (Sprint 2 audit + retire/refactor); orchestrator startup script Wan2GP coupling missed at cleanup (Step 7 §3 enumerates lines 174 + 267-292 + 463).

### Step 9: Self-review checklist before handoff
**Scope:** Small
1. Verify exactly one H1, TOC IDs resolve, every referenced file path resolves under the working tree.
2. Verify the audit table in Step 2 §3 references every task type from the union of `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` ∪ `task_registry.py:1442-1511` dispatch keys, AND the cutover cohort table in Step 6 references the same union. Names match runtime values (`rife_interpolate_images`, not `rife_interpolate`).
3. Verify Hunyuan template absence is identified as a hard gate for Cohort D / Sprint 4.
4. Verify the memory-profile abstraction is described as layering on `SessionConfig.{vram_policy,reserve_vram_gb,cache_policy,disable_smart_memory}` with the corrected citations: `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` (embedded `Configuration`); `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` (managed-server CLI argv).
5. Verify the existing `source/models/comfy/` integration (handler + utils + dispatch + tests) has an explicit retire/refactor decision.
6. Verify the adapter scope explicitly covers both the direct-queue conversion seam and the nested handler-child-task seam.
7. Verify the default-profile-by-environment statement: production = `worker_startup.template.sh:463` `--wgp-profile 1`; dev = `start_worker.bat:14` / `scripts/live_test/main.py:27` / `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27` `--wgp-profile 3`. The plan must not place `worker_startup.template.sh:463` under the profile-3 list.
8. Verify Sprint 8 / Section 6–8 final-removal checklist names **all three** orchestrator startup-template touchpoints: line 174 (FALLBACK_DIR Headless-Wan2GP), lines 267-292 (Wan2GP submodule reconciliation block), line 463 (`--wgp-profile 1`).
9. Confirm no unsourced claims; each capability gap cites either a file path or a documented absence.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1 → 2 → 3 in order (frame, then audit each side).
2. Step 4 depends on the gap rows surfaced by 2+3 and on the existing-Comfy decision (§7).
3. Steps 5, 6, 7 build on 4 and may be drafted in parallel.
4. Step 8 collected throughout, finalized last.
5. Step 9 final pass before handing back to executor.

## Validation Order
1. Executor writes `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` per this plan.
2. Reviewer verifies (a) file exists at exactly `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`; (b) every Step's required table/section is present with named columns; (c) every task type from `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` ∪ dispatch keys in `task_registry.py:1442-1511` (`travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`) is referenced ≥1 time; (d) `rife_interpolate_images` (not `rife_interpolate`) is the runtime name used; (e) memory-profile gap is identified as P0 and described as layering on `SessionConfig` knobs with corrected citations (`session.py:625-656` embedded Configuration; `session.py:663-678` managed-server argv); (f) Hunyuan template absence is a hard gate for its cohort; (g) existing `source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}` has an explicit reuse/extend/retire decision; (h) adapter scope explicitly covers both direct-queue conversion AND nested handler child-task enqueue paths; (i) default-profile statements are environment-correct (prod=1 at `worker_startup.template.sh:463`, dev=3 at `start_worker.bat:14` / `scripts/live_test/{main,smoke}.py:27`); (j) Sprint 8 final-removal checklist enumerates all three orchestrator startup-template touchpoints (line 174, lines 267-292, line 463); (k) sprints ≤2 weeks each; (l) rollback mechanism is concrete; (m) all source-code references resolve.

    "Queue contracts (Supabase task table schema, claim/status update flow, output upload paths) remain unchanged across the migration \u2014 only the in-process generation backend swaps.",
    "`reigh-worker-orchestrator` continues to provision single-GPU RunPod pods using its current spawner; the migration changes only the worker startup command, image, and submodule layout, not the orchestration algorithm.",
    "`reigh-app` (frontend + edge functions) is unaffected; existing per-task UI controls map onto the new backend without API changes.",
    "VibeComfy at `./vibecomfy` is the canonical home for new templates and patches; reigh-worker depends on it as a Python package and does not fork its templates.",
    "The 5-tier memory-profile semantics (Max Performance / High RAM / Balanced / Conservative / Minimum) are preserved as the public contract; display strings in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:605-609` continue to surface in the worker UI/logs even after the underlying mechanism changes to `SessionConfig` overlays.",
    "The proposed `MemoryProfile`\u2192`SessionConfig` mapping is a starting hypothesis; final values come from Sprint 1 measurement against the Wan2GP profile-1 (production) and profile-3 (dev) baselines.",

But v3 critique surfaced a new cluster of 5 flags (FLAG-008, correctness, scope, callers, all_locations, issue_hints) that all converge on a single concrete gap: the Sprint 8 reigh-worker removal checklist is narrower than the actual WGP surface in the repo. Specifically missing: (1) root `reigh-worker/headless_wgp.py`; (2) `reigh-worker/source/runtime/entrypoints/headless_wgp.py`; (3) duplicate `headless_wgp = \"...\"` console-script entries in `reigh-worker/pyproject.toml:109` and `:165`; (4) the WGP test suite beyond `test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py` \u2014 `test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_bridge_ports_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_init_bootstrap_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_output_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_params_overrides.py`, `test_wgp_patch_context_contracts.py`. These are concrete, verifiable additions, not architectural tensions. Iteration Pressure: FG-001/002/004 each show 3 iterations on the same fuzzy theme (final-removal scope), but each is a single flag and no reopens exist \u2014 does not meet the TIEBREAKER threshold (`>=2 member flags across >=2 iterations` requires >=2 flags). Score is climbing (14.5 \u2192 15.5 \u2192 21.0), which is a yellow signal: each iteration the critic line-verifies more of the plan and finds new specific holes. Mitigation in the next iteration: have the plan add both the explicit file list AND a meta-instruction that the executor run a final `git grep -l 'wgp_\\\\|headless_wgp\\\\|mmgp\\\\|Wan2GP'` sweep over `reigh-worker/` to bound the enumeration \u2014 that sets a stable base so iteration 4 isn't another file-naming round.   "signals": {
    "iteration": 3,
    "idea": "Produce a migration plan document for moving reigh-worker's execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy. Repos live side-by-side at ./reigh-worker (current Wan2GP consumer) and ./vibecomfy (target). Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles). (3) Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (memory-profile abstraction modeled on Wan2GP's, equivalents for every task type, model-management lifecycle). (4) Sprint-by-sprint migration plan, each sprint <=2 weeks, sequenced for incremental risk retirement (shadow/dual-run/canary before full cutover), with concrete shippable outcomes. Cover: VibeComfy parity work, adapter/shim in reigh-worker, dual-execution+comparison harness, per-task-type cutover order with rationale, rollback plan, telemetry/observability changes, final Wan2GP removal. (5) Open questions, assumptions, decision points, risks, mitigations. Context: reigh-worker uses Wan2GP today as in-process execution backend across multiple task types (image gen, video gen, edits, etc.). Memory profiles are non-negotiable \u2014 full parity required before cutover. VibeComfy is a Python toolkit around ComfyUI workflows with VibeWorkflow IR, ready_templates, embedded-local + RunPod execution. Migration should preserve reigh-worker's external behavior (queue contracts, output shapes, latency SLOs). Orchestrator (./reigh-worker-orchestrator) provisions GPU workers; coordinate worker-image/runtime changes with that. Output: docs/migration-vibecomfy.md.",

        Requirements:
        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.
        - Update the plan to address the significant issues.
        - Keep the plan readable and executable.
        - Return flags_addressed with the exact flag IDs you addressed.
        - Include `changes_summary` as a short plain-English summary of what changed in the revision. If there were no concrete flags, say that explicitly (for example: `No critique flags were raised; refined wording and kept the plan aligned for execution.`).
        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Each success criterion should include a `requires` field listing the capabilities needed for verification. Valid capability strings: `run_shell`, `read_files`, `run_tests`, `parse_diff`, `read_build_output`, `run_linter` (container), `drive_browser`, `inspect_runtime_ui`, `observe_runtime_logs`, `subjective_judgment`, `verify_physical_device` (human). `must` criteria MUST have non-empty `requires`. Example: `{"criterion": "All tests pass", "priority": "must", "requires": ["run_tests"]}`.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

        Current plan (markdown):
        # Implementation Plan: Migrate reigh-worker execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy

## Overview
Author a migration plan document at `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` covering the cutover of `reigh-worker`'s in-process generation backend from vendored `Wan2GP/wgp.py` (orchestrated via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) to `vibecomfy` (Python toolkit around ComfyUI, with `VibeWorkflow` IR, `ready_templates/`, embedded + RunPod runtimes in `vibecomfy/runtime/`).

Key constraints discovered while scoping (verified against the working tree):
- **Memory profiles** are non-negotiable. Today: `--wgp-profile {1..5}` in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:556-566` mapping to mmgp's `force_profile_no` / `default_profile`, plus per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`. VibeComfy already exposes lower-level memory controls in `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:46-49` (`SessionConfig.vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`). Translation paths verified: `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` builds the embedded ComfyUI `Configuration`; `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` builds the managed-server CLI argv. The 1–5 tier abstraction is missing — it must layer on top of the existing `SessionConfig` surface.
- **Default profile is environment-specific.** Production = `--wgp-profile 1` via `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463`. Dev = `--wgp-profile 3` via `start_worker.bat:14`, `scripts/live_test/main.py:27`, `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27`. Sprint 0 baselines both.
- **Task surface to preserve** is the union of `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`, the 14 dispatch keys at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:1442-1511` (`travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`), and friendly aliases at `source/core/log/display_names.py:8-39` (`rife_interpolate` is alias-only; runtime name is `rife_interpolate_images`).
- **Existing ComfyUI integration** at `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}` is refactored (not duplicated): `comfy_handler.py` rewritten to delegate to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; `comfy_utils.py` retired; new sibling `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` is the only added file.
- **Coupling beyond generation**: `WanOrchestrator` handles dynamic model definition load, LoRA setup, Uni3C ControlNet caching, RIFE temporal interpolation, Qwen prompt expander, Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotators, save-video helpers — re-exported through `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` and `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`.
- **Wan2GP surface inside reigh-worker is broader than the wgp_* directories.** Verified inventory: root scripts `reigh-worker/headless_wgp.py` and `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py`; entrypoint shims `source/runtime/entrypoints/headless_wgp.py` and `source/runtime/entrypoints/headless_model_management.py`; duplicate console-script registrations at `reigh-worker/pyproject.toml:109` and `:165` (both `headless_wgp = "source.runtime.entrypoints.headless_wgp:WanOrchestrator"`); WGP-specific test suite — 7 files: `test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_bridge_ports_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_init_bootstrap_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_output_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_params_overrides.py`, `test_wgp_patch_context_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py`.
- **Orchestrator coupling extends beyond `--wgp-profile`**: `worker_startup.template.sh` references Wan2GP at line 174 + 179-183 (`FALLBACK_DIR="$WORKSPACE_DIR/Headless-Wan2GP"`), lines 267-292 (Wan2GP submodule reconciliation), and line 463 (`--wgp-profile 1`).
- **VibeComfy ready templates** at `vibecomfy/ready_templates/{image,video,edit,audio}/` count **50 `.py` files** (live `find`; README's "46" is stale). No Hunyuan template — P0 hard gate for Cohort D.

The deliverable is a planning markdown doc; no code changes in this phase. The executor will write to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`.

## Main Phase

**Scope:** Small

2. State goals (preserve external worker contract: queue payloads, output shapes, per-task SLOs, memory-profile guarantees) and explicit non-goals (no Supabase queue schema rework, no orchestrator algorithmic changes beyond image/runtime swap, no `reigh-app` UI/API changes).
3. Reference authoritative source paths: `reigh-worker/source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}`, `reigh-worker/source/core/log/display_names.py`, `reigh-worker/headless_wgp.py`, `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/entrypoints/{headless_wgp.py,headless_model_management.py}`, `reigh-worker/pyproject.toml:109,165`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/{run.py,session.py,server.py,client.py}`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`, `vibecomfy/docs/runtime_surface.md`.

Note the alternate entrypoint `headless_wgp` registered as a console script in `pyproject.toml:109,165` resolving to `source.runtime.entrypoints.headless_wgp:WanOrchestrator`, and the root convenience scripts `reigh-worker/headless_wgp.py` and `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py` that are wrapped by the entrypoint shims at `source/runtime/entrypoints/{headless_wgp.py,headless_model_management.py}`.

Include an "alias for" column so `rife_interpolate` does not leak forward; runtime name is `rife_interpolate_images`.

### Step 3: Audit VibeComfy capabilities (Section 2)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document `VibeWorkflow` IR (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/workflow.py`): `add_node`/`connect`/`disconnect`/`replace_edge`/`finalize_metadata`/`compile("api")` per `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`. Rule: changes-handles → template, decorates-handles → patch.
2. Document `ready_templates/` organization. Use the live `find vibecomfy/ready_templates -name '*.py' | wc -l` count of **50** (verified) and explicitly note the README's "46" is stale. Map known templates to direct-queue task types. Mark `hunyuan` as a missing-template gap.
3. Document the runtime: `run_embedded`/`run_embedded_sync` (`runtime/run.py`) over `EmbeddedSession` (`runtime/session.py`); `run`/`run_sync` against an external Comfy server (`runtime/{client.py,server.py}`).
4. Document **existing memory controls** on `SessionConfig` (`session.py:46-49`) and the two translation paths:
   - `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` builds the embedded ComfyUI `Configuration` dict (`highvram`/`lowvram`/`normalvram`, `reserve_vram`, `cache_classic`/`cache_none`/`cache_lru`, `disable_smart_memory`, then `config.extra` and `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env JSON merge).
   - `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` builds the managed-server CLI argv (`--highvram`/`--lowvram`/`--normalvram`, `--reserve-vram`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic`/`--cache-none`/`--cache-lru N`, `--port`).
5. Document validation: `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/schema/{validate.py,cache.py,format.py,provider.py}` plus `vibecomfy doctor`.
6. Document RunPod path: `runpod-lifecycle` via `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py`. For reigh-worker, `EmbeddedSession` is the analogue of vendored `wgp.py`.
7. Document explicit gaps: no 1–5 profile tier abstraction (only the lower-level knobs in §4); no LoRA-key sanitizer equivalent; no Uni3C ControlNet cache; no RIFE temporal interpolation helper; no Qwen prompt expander wrapper; no Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotator re-exports; no Wan2GP `save_video` callable; no Hunyuan ready_template.

### Step 4: Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (Section 3)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Author "Parity Gap Matrix" table (cols: capability, current Wan2GP location, VibeComfy current state, required work, P-priority, owner-repo, target sprint).
2. Specify **memory-profile abstraction (P0)** as a thin overlay on existing `SessionConfig` knobs (Step 3 §4). New `vibecomfy.runtime.profile` module with `MemoryProfile` enum (1–5) and `MemoryProfile.to_session_overrides()` returning a partial dict overlaying `vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`. Both `_embedded_configuration_for_session` (`session.py:625-656`) and `_comfy_server_argv` (`session.py:663-678`) consume the resulting `SessionConfig` unchanged. Concrete starting mapping (Sprint-1 tuned): MAX_PERFORMANCE → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; HIGH_RAM → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="lru:32"`; BALANCED → `vram_policy="normal"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; CONSERVATIVE → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="classic"`, `reserve_vram_gb=2.0`; MINIMUM → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="none"`, `disable_smart_memory=True`, `reserve_vram_gb=4.0`. Per-call `override_profile` mirrors `wgp_params.py` semantics. Acceptance: per-profile parity smoke tests against profile-1 (prod) and profile-3 (dev) baselines.
3. Specify **task-type → VibeComfy template registry**: lives at `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (only genuinely new file). Covers the union task surface — direct-queue catalog plus orchestrated-handler internal sub-tasks.
4. **Missing-template gaps with explicit P-priority and go/no-go**:
   - **Hunyuan (P0 for Cohort D / Sprint 4)** — no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` exists. Cohort D blocked until template ships. Owner: VibeComfy maintainer; target: Sprint 4.
   - Any further gaps from Step 3 §2 mapping table get explicit go/no-go gate per cohort.
5. **Vendor-utility shims**: RIFE keeps vendored under `reigh-worker/source/media/`; Uni3C as VibeComfy patch on Wan2.2 templates; Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow pre-process before workflow build; Qwen prompt expander pre-process; LoRA-key sanitizer becomes a VibeComfy patch over `LoraLoader` nodes.
6. **Dynamic-model-definition** decision: recommend build-time freeze; Open Question Q1.
7. **Existing `source/models/comfy/` decision**: refactor `comfy_handler.py` to delegate via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; retire `comfy_utils.py`; add `template_routing.py` (only new file). Migrate test imports.
8. **Observability shim**: translate `RunResult` (`run_id`, `prompt_id`, `outputs`, `metadata_path`, `log_path`) into existing telemetry (heartbeat logs, `system_logs`, `source/core/log/debug_card.py`).

### Step 5: Sprint-by-sprint migration plan (Section 4)
**Scope:** Large
1. Sprint table (≤2 wk each), each listing: goals, shippable artifacts, exit criteria, owner, risk, rollback move.
   - **Sprint 0 (1 wk)** Discovery freeze + benchmark harness via `scripts/run_worker_matrix.py` + `scripts/live_test/`. Output: `reigh-worker/docs/migration-baselines.md` with separate baselines for prod profile-1 and dev profile-3. Live task-surface inventory (`task_registry.py:1442-1511` ∪ `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`) and live VibeComfy template inventory.
   - **Sprint 1 (2 wk)** VibeComfy memory-profile MVP. Exit: profiles 1–5 round-trip on `image/z_image` and `video/wan_t2v` with measured VRAM/wall-clock at parity-or-better than `--wgp-profile {1..5}` for both prod-profile-1 and dev-profile-3 reference points.
   - **Sprint 2 (2 wk)** Adapter shim — refactor existing `source/models/comfy/`. Rewrite `comfy_handler.py` to call `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; add `template_routing.py`; retire `comfy_utils.py`; migrate test imports. Feature flag `REIGH_BACKEND={wgp|comfy}` threaded through both seams (`_handle_direct_queue_task` and `context["task_queue"]`). Wire safest tasks first: `z_image_turbo`, `qwen_image_2512`, `t2v`. Exit: feature-flagged Comfy path runs end-to-end in dev plus a `travel_segment` smoke run that enqueues a `t2v` child.
   - **Sprint 3 (2 wk)** Dual-execution + comparison harness `scripts/dual_run_compare.py` (image hash, video frame-pHash, frame count, dimensions, audio length, latency, VRAM, OOM count). Nightly across the Sprint 0 matrix. Exit: green report ≥80% of task types.
   - **Sprint 4 (2 wk)** Wan-family + **Hunyuan (P0 hard gate)**: ship `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` with memory-profile mapping. Wan: `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`. Exit: dual-run parity green for Wan family + Hunyuan template runnable with each profile.
   - **Sprint 5 (2 wk)** LTX, Flux, Qwen parity + move pre-processing pipeline. Exit: dual-run parity green for remaining direct-queue tasks.
   - **Sprint 6 (2 wk)** Orchestrated handlers covering both seams: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`. Exit: full union task surface runnable through Comfy backend on both adapter seams.
   - **Sprint 7 (2 wk)** Canary in production by `task_type` cohort using server-side selector. Hold each cohort 48 h before promoting next.
   - **Sprint 8 (1 wk)** Wan2GP removal (full enumeration in Step 7 §3, plus mandatory `git grep` sweep at Step 9 §10).
2. Critical path callout: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3, then 4/5 in parallel, then 6 → 7 → 8.

### Step 6: Per-task-type cutover order with rationale (Section 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Cohort table covering the union task surface:
   - **A** lowest risk, image-only deterministic: `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_2512`, `flux`, `wan_2_2_t2i`.
   - **B** medium, image edits: `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`.
   - **C** medium-high, video gen: `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `generate_video`.
   - **D** high, VACE + Hunyuan: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `hunyuan` — gated on Hunyuan ready_template.
   - **E** highest, orchestrated + raw-Comfy: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
2. Canary mechanism: server-side `backend_for_task_type` map read at task claim time. Cohort E respects parent backend selection AND the child-task seam (Sprint 6).

### Step 7: Rollback plan, telemetry/observability, final removal (Sections 6–8)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Rollback: `REIGH_BACKEND` flag (process level via `--backend wgp|comfy`) plus per-task-type override; both stacks coexist in the worker image until Sprint 8; orchestrator passes flag through `worker_startup.template.sh`. Trigger conditions: latency > p95 baseline by N%, error-class spike, output-divergence rate > threshold. Adapter scope explicitly covers both seams.
2. Telemetry: add `backend` and `template_id` log labels; emit `vibecomfy.run_id` and `comfy.prompt_id`; mirror VRAM logging from `wgp/generators/output.py:log_memory_stats` to Comfy path; capture `RunResult.log_path` into `source/core/log/debug_card.py`.

   - **reigh-worker WGP test suite — all 7 files:** `tests/test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py`, `tests/test_wgp_bridge_ports_contracts.py`, `tests/test_wgp_init_bootstrap_contracts.py`, `tests/test_wgp_output_contracts.py`, `tests/test_wgp_params_overrides.py`, `tests/test_wgp_patch_context_contracts.py`, `tests/test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py`. Plus any architecture/coverage tests that import retired WGP surfaces (surfaced by the `git grep` sweep, see Step 9 §10).
   - **reigh-worker existing-Comfy refactor residue:** `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` (already retired in Sprint 2; verify removed).
   - **reigh-worker-orchestrator startup template `worker_startup.template.sh`:**

### Step 8: Open questions, assumptions, risks/mitigations (Section 9)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open questions: Q1 static vs dynamic VibeComfy template authoring; Q2 5-tier `MemoryProfile`→`SessionConfig` overlay process-restart feasibility; Q3 dual-run divergence threshold; Q4 preprocessing annotators in `reigh-worker` vs `vibecomfy_extras/`; Q5 RunPod startup-time / `disk_size_gb` impact; Q6 LoRA-key sanitizer mmgp-specific vs ComfyUI portable; Q7 RIFE / Uni3C vendor location; Q8 Hunyuan ready_template ownership; Q9 fate of `comfy` task type; Q10 dev/prod default-profile alignment; **Q11** whether `headless_model_management` (separate from `headless_wgp`) has any non-WGP callers we need to migrate vs delete.
2. Assumptions: queue contracts and Supabase schema unchanged; `reigh-app` unaffected; `vibecomfy` is canonical home for new templates; the 5-tier display contract is preserved; default profile diverges by env (prod=1, dev=3); both `pyproject.toml` `headless_wgp` entries (lines 109 and 165) are duplicate registrations (one in `[project.scripts]`, one likely in `[tool.poetry.scripts]` or section block) and both must be removed.
3. Risks + mitigations: missing 1–5 profile tier (Sprint 1 P0); embedded ComfyUI cold-boot (long-lived `EmbeddedSession`); template catalog drift (template-routing tests); Hunyuan template absence (Sprint 4 hard gate); LoRA divergence (golden-output corpus); pod disk too small during dual-run (Sprint 0 baseline); orchestrated child-task seam missed by adapter (Sprint 2 dual-seam wiring); existing `source/models/comfy` integration drift (Sprint 2 retire/refactor); orchestrator startup-script Wan2GP coupling missed at cleanup (Step 7 §3 enumeration); **residual WGP entrypoints/tests left after directory deletion (Step 7 §3 explicit per-file list + Step 9 §10 mandatory `git grep` sweep).**

    Any file the sweep surfaces that is **not** already in Step 7 §3 must be added to the deletion/migration list before Sprint 8 closes. After the deletions, the same sweep must return zero hits (excluding the migration doc itself and any historical `CHANGELOG`/`docs/` entries explicitly retained for archive).
11. Confirm no unsourced claims; each capability gap cites either a file path or a documented absence.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1 → 2 → 3 in order (frame, then audit each side).
2. Step 4 depends on the gap rows surfaced by 2+3 and on the existing-Comfy decision (§7).
3. Steps 5, 6, 7 build on 4 and may be drafted in parallel.
4. Step 8 collected throughout, finalized last.
5. Step 9 final pass before handing back to executor — including the `git grep` sweep verification step.

  "signals": {
    "iteration": 4,
    "idea": "Produce a migration plan document for moving reigh-worker's execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy. Repos live side-by-side at ./reigh-worker (current Wan2GP consumer) and ./vibecomfy (target). Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles). (3) Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (memory-profile abstraction modeled on Wan2GP's, equivalents for every task type, model-management lifecycle). (4) Sprint-by-sprint migration plan, each sprint <=2 weeks, sequenced for incremental risk retirement (shadow/dual-run/canary before full cutover), with concrete shippable outcomes. Cover: VibeComfy parity work, adapter/shim in reigh-worker, dual-execution+comparison harness, per-task-type cutover order with rationale, rollback plan, telemetry/observability changes, final Wan2GP removal. (5) Open questions, assumptions, decision points, risks, mitigations. Context: reigh-worker uses Wan2GP today as in-process execution backend across multiple task types (image gen, video gen, edits, etc.). Memory profiles are non-negotiable \u2014 full parity required before cutover. VibeComfy is a Python toolkit around ComfyUI workflows with VibeWorkflow IR, ready_templates, embedded-local + RunPod execution. Migration should preserve reigh-worker's external behavior (queue contracts, output shapes, latency SLOs). Orchestrator (./reigh-worker-orchestrator) provisions GPU workers; coordinate worker-image/runtime changes with that. Output: docs/migration-vibecomfy.md.",

   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Step 9 §10 now reflects that `reigh-worker/`, `reigh-worker-orchestrator/`, and `vibecomfy/` are independent Git repositories nested in the workspace, and provides two verbatim invocations: Option A (`git -C reigh-worker grep -lE '…'` and `git -C reigh-worker-orchestrator grep -lE '…'` per repo, with path-prefix `sed`) for pre-Sprint-8 committed-file discovery, and Option B (`rg -l '…' reigh-worker/ reigh-worker-orchestrator/`) for the post-deletion zero-hits assertion that catches untracked files too. The doc explicitly notes that workspace-root `git grep` would silently skip the nested repos. The Overview, Step 8 §2 (assumption), Step 8 §3 (risk), and Validation Order item (l) each anchor the per-repo invariant. No other plan content changed.
